class="league-title">No Games</h2></div><p class="rotoql-content-article__paragraph"><strong>About BetQL</strong></p><p class="rotoql-content-article__paragraph">BetQL is a sports-betting analytics platform designed to help bettors identify value, track trends, and make informed wagers across all major sports. We use our state-of-the-art computer model to analyze every bet, every day to find mis-priced lines. By combining robust data modeling, real-time odds monitoring, expert insight, and quantitative trends, we aim to elevate both casual and serious bettors from guesswork to strategy.</p><p class="rotoql-content-article__paragraph"><strong>Types of Betting Predictions</strong></p><p class="rotoql-content-article__paragraph">We offer a variety of betting predictions including moneyline, spread and over/under picks for all major sports. All BetQL computer model predictions created using a proprietary algorithm designed to identify trends and patterns that can help you make informed betting decisions. Each game or match is simulated 10,000 times based on all available data providing BetQL subscribers with a fact based prediction and clear probability projections.</p><p class="rotoql-content-article__paragraph"><strong>Computer Analyzed Prediction Program</strong></p><p class="rotoql-content-article__paragraph">Every bet on BetQL receives a <a href="https://support.betql.co/hc/en-us/articles/360047973014-BetQL-Model-Bets-Understanding-Star-Ratings" rel="noopener nofollow" target="_blank">star rating</a> based on historical data, matchup details, homefield advantage, weather, injuries, and narratives. Bets are rated on a scale of 1-5 stars indicating their advantage for the bettor. The higher the number of stars the bigger the edge. As external factors (e.g., a new injury or favorable weather shift) alter betting lines, BetQL updates our star ratings throughout the day to reflect real-time opportunities.</p><p class="rotoql-content-article__paragraph"><strong>Exclusive Public Betting Data</strong></p><p class="rotoql-content-article__paragraph">In addition to the standard computer based moneyline, spread and over/under predictions, BetQl also provides its users with public and expert betting data. This allows bettors to gauge public sentiment towards a specific wager as well as see where the sharps (experts) think the odds makers may have made a mistake and are taking the underdog. Our “trending bets” section gives bettors an overview of our computer model, sharp betting and the BetQL community&#x27;s prediction all in one place. Find the latest public betting percentages for all of today&#x27;s top games and go with the herd or fade the public. </p><p class="rotoql-content-article__paragraph"><strong>Specialized Sports Predictions</strong></p><p class="rotoql-content-article__paragraph">Each sport has its own dedicated section on the BetQL site where bettors can find predictions for sports specific wages such as <a href="/mlb/odds/first-five-innings-odds">MLB first five innings</a>, <a href="/nba/odds/first-half-lines">NBA first half</a> or <a href="/nfl/prop-picks">NFL player prop bets</a>. Our line movement tracking feature allows you to see how the betting odds have changed since their announcement and are updated every 5 minutes to give you the latest odds. The game/match analysis gives complete breakdowns of the matchup including historic trends, projected winning percentages as well as the latest lines from top sportsbooks.</p><p class="rotoql-content-article__paragraph"><strong>Daily News from BetQL Sports Experts</strong></p><p class="rotoql-content-article__paragraph">In addition to the latest sports betting tips, we&#x27;ve compiled a team of sports betting experts who create daily news articles where they share their top picks and insights.
